Keep\nthe scalar body and add a kernel in front of it:\n\n```cpp\n#include \"../dsp/simd.hpp\"\n\nnamespace kernels {\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\nCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void mul_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept {\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8)\n        _mm256_store_ps(out + i, _mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_load_ps(a + i), _mm256_load_ps(b + i)));\n}\n#endif\n}\n\ninline void op_mul(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    // ... resolve a, b, out ...\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) { kernels::mul_avx2(a, b, out); return; }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) out[i] = a[i] * b[i];  // fallback stays\n}\n```\n\n- `CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2` is `[[gnu::target(\"avx2\")]]`; no compiler flags change.\n  FMA is **not** enabled (it would change rounding).\n- Buffers from `ctx.buffers->get()` are 32-byte aligned; `BLOCK_SIZE` is 128.\n- You may also add NEON (`CEDAR_SIMD_NEON`) or WASM SIMD128\n  (`CEDAR_SIMD_WASM`) paths; they are measured later on other machines, not\n  gated here.\n\n## Rules (enforced by the verifier \u2014 violations are rejected automatically)\n\n1. Output must be **bit-identical** to the original for every opcode.\n   No approximations are allowed for these opcodes.\n4.       // 0-1 phase within current beat (= cycle)\n    62\t    float bar_phase = 0.0f;        // 0-1 phase within current bar (= beat = cycle)\n    63\t\n    64\t    // Update derived timing values\n    65\t    void update_timing() {\n    66\t        float samples_per_beat = (60.0f / bpm) * sample_rate;\n    67\t        float sample_in_beat = static_cast<float>(global_sample_counter % static_cast<std::uint64_t>(samples_per_beat));\n    68\t        beat_phase = sample_in_beat / samples_per_beat;\n    69\t        bar_phase = beat_phase;\n    70\t    }\n    71\t\n    72\t    // Set sample rate and update derived values\n    73\t    void set_sample_rate(float rate) {\n    74\t        sample_rate = rate;\n    75\t        inv_sample_rate = 1.0f / rate;\n    76\t    }\n    77\t\n    78\t    // Timing helper methods for sequencing opcodes\n    79\t    [[nodiscard]] float samples_per_beat() const noexcept {\n    80\t        return (60.0f / bpm) * sample_rate;\n    81\t    }\n    82\t\n    83\t    [[nodiscard]] float samples_per_bar() const noexcept {\n    84\t        return samples_per_beat();\n    85\t    }\n    86\t\n    87\t    [[nodiscard]] float samples_per_cycle() const noexcept {\n    88\t        return samples_per_beat(); Every seed is guaranteed one\n    78\t    // near-denormal noise segment (1e-35..1e-25) and one hot one (2-4x),\n    79\t    // at random positions, so that coverage never depends on luck.\n    80\t    void make_hidden(std::mt19937& rng) {\n    81\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n    82\t        constexpr std::size_t seg = STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE / 8;\n    83\t        const auto tiny_seg = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 8.0f) % 8;\n    84\t        const auto hot_seg = (tiny_seg + 1 + static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 7.0f) % 7) % 8;\n    85\t        for (std::size_t s = 0; s < 8; ++s) {\n    86\t            int kind = static_cast<int>(u(rng) * 4.0f);\n    87\t            float level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -30.0f * u(rng) * u(rng)) * (1.0f + 3.0f * u(rng));\n    88\t            if (s == tiny_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -25.0f - 10.0f * u(rng)); }\n    89\t            if (s == hot_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * (2.0f + 2.0f * u(rng)); }\n    90\t            float dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f);\n    91\t            for (std::size_t k = s * seg; k < (s + 1) * seg; ++k) {\n    92\t                switch (kind) {\n    93\t                    case 0: noise[k] *= level; break;\n    94\t                    case 1: noise[k] = u(rng) < 0.002f ? level * (u(rng) < 0.5f ? -1.0f : 1.0f) : 0.0f; break;\n    95\t                    case 2: noise[k] = (k % 4096 == 0) ? State is shared across passes.\n   503\t        // Inner dispatch goes back through execute_step so nested meta-ops\n   504\t        // (BLOCK_CALL etc.) inside the LOOP_STATIC body work too.\n   505\t        const std::size_t body_len = inst.rate;\n   506\t        const std::uint16_t iterations = inst.out_buffer;\n   507\t        for (std::uint16_t it = 0; it < iterations; ++it) {\n   508\t            std::size_t inner = ip + 1;\n   509\t            const std::size_t inner_end = ip + 1 + body_len;\n   510\t            while (inner < inner_end) {\n   511\t                inner = execute_step(slot, body, inner);\n   512\t            }\n   513\t        }\n   514\t        return ip + body_len + 1;\n   515\t      }\n   516\t      case Opcode::BLOCK_CALL:\n   517\t        // L2: dispatch a shared user-`fn` body from the subprogram table.\n   518\t        // The body is not inline \u2014 advance by one. `slot` must be non-null\n   519\t        // for BLOCK_CALL to resolve; nullptr means the body came from a\n   520\t        // legacy non-slot codepath and codegen guarantees no BLOCK_CALL.\n   521\t        if (slot != nullptr) {\n   522\t            execute_block_call(slot, body, ip);\n   523\t        }\n   524\t        return ip + 1;\n   525\t      case Opcode::BLOCK_BIND: {\n   526\t        // L2 >5-param call: a contiguous run of BLOCK_BIND carries the\n   527\t        // logical param slots >=5 for the BLOCK_CALL that terminates the\n   528\t        // run. Scan the run, then dispatch the BLOCK_CALL with the binds.\n   529\t        const std::size_t bind_start = ip;\n   530\t        const std::size_t end = body.size();\n   531\t        std::size_t scan = ip;\n   532\t        while (scan < end && body[scan].opcode == Opcode::BLOCK_BIND) {\n   533\t            ++scan;\n   534\t        }\n   535\t        assert(scan < end && body[scan].opcode == Opcode::BLOCK_CALL &&\n   536\t               \"BLOCK_BIND run not terminated by BLOCK_CALL\");\n   537\t        if (slot != nullptr && scan < end &&\n   538\t            body[scan].opcode == Opcode::BLOCK_CALL) {\n   539\t            execute_block_call(slot, body, scan,\n   540\t                               body.subspan(bind_start, scan - bind_start));\n   541\t        }\n   542\t        return scan + 1;\n   543\t      }\n   544\t      case Opcode::RET:\n   545\t        // RET terminates a subprogram body. Body runners bound themselves\n   546\t        // by body.size(), so reaching the final instruction naturally is\n   547\t        // the normal exit \u2014 a mid-body RET means codegen emitted stray\n   548\t        // control flow. In release, advancing to body.size() ends the loop.\n   549\t        assert(ip + 1 == body.size() && \"stray RET inside body\");\n   550\t        return body.size();\n   551\t      default:\n   552\t        execute(inst);\n   553\t        return ip + 1;\n   554\t    }\n   555\t}\n   556\t\n   557\tvoid VM::execute_program(const ProgramSlot* slot, float* out_left, float* out_right) {\n   558\t    // Set output buffer pointers\n   559\t    ctx_.output_left = out_left;\n   560\t    ctx_.output_right = out_right;\n   561\t\n   562\t    // Mark beginning of frame for state GC tracking\n   563\t    state_pool_.begin_frame();\n   564\t\n   565\t    // Execute all instructions (index-based for POLY block jumping).\n   566\t    // The program span covers [ main | subprogram bodies ]; the main dispatch\n   567\t    // loop runs only the main region \u2014 block bodies are reached via\n   568\t    // FOREACH_EVENT / BLOCK_CALL dispatch, never fallen into.\n   569\t    auto program = slot->program();\n   570\t    // With no subprogram table the whole span is main program (back-compat,\n   571\t    // including ProgramSlots constructed without load()). With a table, the\n   572\t    // main loop stops at the main/body boundary.\n   573\t    const std::size_t main_end = (slot->block_count == 0)\n   574\t                                     ? program.size()\n   575\t                                     : static_cast<std::size_t>(slot->main_count);\n   576\t    auto main_span = program.subspan(0, main_end);\n   577\t    std::size_t ip = 0;\n   578\t    while (ip < main_end) {\n   579\t        const Instruction& inst = main_span[ip];\n   580\t        if (inst.opcode == Opcode::POLY_BEGIN) {\n   581\t            ip = execute_poly_block(slot, program, ip);\n   582\t        } else if (inst.opcode == Opcode::FOREACH_EVENT) {\n   583\t            // L3 dispatch-loop opcode: run the subprogram body from the\n   584\t            // table. The body is not inline \u2014 advance by exactly one.\n   585\t            execute_foreach_event(slot, program, ip);\n   586\t            ++ip;\n   587\t        } else if (inst.opcode == Opcode::EVENT_MAP) {\n   588\t            // prd-runtime-event-transforms: closure form needs slot->blocks[],\n   589\t            // so it dispatches here; the Phase-1 packed form has no body."
